<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">With the arrival of Scarlet &amp; Violet in March, we’ll have a handful of new deck archetypes joining the fray—and with rotation, the meta will be shaken up quite thoroughly. As always, some deck archetypes are better than others—some are meme tier and some are merely playable, fun decks without much competitive promise. Lists below are very rough drafts and are intended as a starting place for testing, not as tournament-ready deck lists. Consider <a href="/guide/proxies">creating and using proxies to test cards you don’t already own</a> prior to making any purchases.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Below is a list of archetypes that may be reasonably playable after this year’s rotation—archetypes from competitive standouts to enjoyable casual archetypes are listed below.</p>
</div>

</div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1671501147277_270580"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Evolve Serperior V into Serperior VSTAR to prepare it to attack and Kricketot into Kricketune to take advantage of its Swelling Tune Ability, which reads, “Your [G] Pokémon in play, except any Kricketune, get +40 HP. You can't apply more than 1 Swelling Tune Ability at a time.” Further increase the difficulty of knocking out your own Serperior VSTAR with Full Face Guard and with Radiant Gardevoir whose ability reduces the damage done to your Pokémon by Pokémon V by 20. Attack using Serperior VSTAR’s Regal Blender attack, which does 190 damage and gives you the opportunity to move any Energy cards attach to your Pokémon to your other Pokémon in any way you like. When advantageous, use Serperior VSTAR’s Star Winder attack, which does 60 damage for each Energy card attached to Serperior VSTAR.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="/highlights/ss12#block-yui_3_17_2_1_1660315435813_50675">Serperior VSTAR SIT 8</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BST/123">Cheryl</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ASR/143">Gardenia’s Vigor</a> as an option to draw a couple of cards while also attaching any up to 2 Grass Energy you draw into or have in your hand to 1 of your Benched Pokémon.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Building Serperior VSTAR with the Lost Engine, making it possible to power up Serperior VSTAR with Mirage Gate.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SIT/169">V Guard Energy</a> as yet another means of reducing the damage dealt to your Serperior VSTAR.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ASR/150">Roxanne</a> as a means of disrupting your opponent in the mid to late game.</p></li></ul>
</div>




















  
  



</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1671501147277_373870"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">EXAMPLE DECK LIST</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 19<br></strong>4 Serperior V SIT 7<br>4 Swablu SIT 142<br>3 Serperior VSTAR SIT 8<br>2 Altaria EVS 106<br>2 Altaria SIT 143<br>1 Kricketot ASR 9<br>1 Kricketune ASR 10<br>1 Manaphy BRS 41<br>1 Radiant Greninja ASR 46</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 30<br></strong>4 Professor's Research SVI 190<br>2 Boss's Orders BRS 132<br>2 Judge SVI 176<br>1 Cheryl BST 123<br>1 Klara CRE 145<br>4 Nest Ball SVI 181<br>4 Trekking Shoes CRZ 145<br>4 Ultra Ball SVI 196<br>1 Escape Rope BST 125<br>1 Pal Pad SVI 182<br>1 Switch SVI 194<br>2 Full Face Guard EVS 148<br>3 Path to the Peak CRE 148</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 11<br></strong>7 Grass Energy SWSHEnergy 1<br>4 Double Turbo Energy BRS 151</p>
</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;"> BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Using Nest Ball, Ultra Ball, and Mesagoza, set up Leafeon V to evolve into Leafeon VMAX and Tarountula to evolve into Spidops ex, whose Ability increases the Retreat Cost of your opponent’s Active Pokémon by 1 [C]. Attack, as possible, with Leafeon VMAX’s Grass Knot, which does 60 damage for each [C] in your opponent’s Active Pokémon’s Retreat Cost. Alternatively, attack using Spidops ex’s Wire Hang, which does 90 damage plus 30 more damage for each [C] in your opponent’s Active Pokémon’s Retreat Cost.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/EVS/8">Leafeon VMAX EVS 8</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SV1V/8?translate=en">Spidops ex SVI 19</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Pokémon Search Cards (<a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SUM/123">Nest Ball</a>, <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BRS/150">Ultra Ball</a>, <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SV1V/78?translate=en">Mesagoza</a>)</p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRZ/14">Leafeon VSTAR CRZ 14</a> as an additional gusting option and alternate two-prize attacker.</p></li></ul>
</div>




















  
  



</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1673643764670_379565"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">EXAMPLE DECK LIST</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 19<br></strong>4 Spidops ex SVI 19<br>4 Tarountula SVI 16<br>3 Leafeon V EVS 7<br>3 Leafeon VMAX EVS 8<br>2 Zoroark EVS 103<br>2 Zorua EVS 102<br>1 Mightyena ASR 96</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 32<br></strong>4 Professor's Research SVI 190<br>2 Boss's Orders BRS 132<br>2 Raihan CRZ 140<br>2 Serena SIT 164<br>4 Nest Ball SVI 181<br>4 Ultra Ball SVI 196<br>3 Pokégear 3.0 SVI 186<br>3 Trekking Shoes CRZ 145<br>2 Switch SVI 194<br>1 Escape Rope BST 125<br>1 Choice Belt BRS 135<br>1 Forest Seal Stone SIT 156<br>3 Mesagoza SVI 178</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 9<br></strong>9 Grass Energy SWSHEnergy 1</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Testing Streams</h3>
</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Using a combination of Magma Basin and manual attachments, power up Entei V’s Burning Rondo attack, which does 20 damage plus 20 more damage for each Benched Pokémon (yours and your opponent’s). Meanwhile play down and evolve Charcadet into Armarouge and Growlithe into Arcanine ex, preparing the Arcanine ex to take over after Entei V goes down. Use Arcanine ex’s Bright Flame attack to do 250 damage to your opponent’s Active Pokémon. As needed, use Raihan, Exp. Share, and Armarouge’s Fire Alarm Ability to help power up your chain of attackers.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BRS/22">Entei V BRS 22</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SV1V/16?translate=en">Arcanine ex SVI 32</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRZ/20">Radiant Charizard PGO 11</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Energy Acceleration Options (<a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SV1S/12?translate=en">Armarouge</a>, <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BST/126">Exp. Share</a>, <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BRS/144">Magma Basin</a>, <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards?q=Raihan">Raihan</a>)</p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SIT/22">Rapidash SIT 22</a> to take advantage of its Heat Boost Ability, which for the discard of a [R] Energy, boosts the damage your [R] Pokémon’s attacks do to your opponent’s Active Pokémon.</p></li></ul>
</div>




















  
  



</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1670431942680_68581"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">

Share SVI 174<br>1 Choice Belt BRS 135<br>1 Forest Seal Stone SIT 156<br>4 Magma Basin BRS 144</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 11<br></strong>11 Fire Energy SWSHEnergy 2</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">ALTERNATE LISTS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=_9ZsqHx01no">KatanaTCG’s version of this archetype</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Testing Streams</h3>

</div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1674655273413_325679"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Play down Magikarp and Origin Forme Palkia V and evolve them into Gyarados ex and Origin Forme Palkia VSTAR, respectively. Discard Water Energy to make it possible to use Origin Forme Palkia VSTAR’s Star Portal to attach Water Energy to Gyarados ex and use its Tyrannical Tail attack which does 180 damage plus 180 more if your opponent’s Active Pokémon already has any damage counters on it. To get damage counters where they’re needed, play down Radiant Alakazam to use its Painful Spoons Ability to move damage counters onto your opponent’s Active Pokémon or use Hawlucha’s Flying Entry to put a damage counter each on two of your opponent’s benched Pokémon.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SV1S/14?translate=en">Gyarados ex SVI 45</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SV1S/45?translate=en">Hawlucha SVI 119</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ASR/40">Origin Forme Palkia VSTAR ASR 40</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Building Gyarados ex with <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/EVS/43">Cryogonal EVS 43</a> instead of Origin Forme Palkia VSTAR to use its Elemental Chain attack to power up Gyarados ex’s pricy attack costs.</p></li></ul>
</div>




















  
  



</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1674655273413_292477"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">EXAMPLE DECK LIST</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 13<br></strong>3 Magikarp PGO 21<br>3 Origin Forme Palkia V ASR 39<br>2 Gyarados ex SVI 45<br>2 Origin Forme Palkia VSTAR ASR 40<br>1 Hawlucha SVI 119<br>1 Lumineon V BRS 40<br>1 Radiant Alakazam SIT 59</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 34<br></strong>4 Professor's Research SVI 190<br>3 Irida ASR 147<br>2 Boss's Orders BRS 132<br>2 Raihan CRZ 140<br>1 Serena SIT 164<br>4 Trekking Shoes CRZ 145<br>4 Ultra Ball SVI 196<br>3 Nest Ball SVI 181<br>2 Escape Rope BST 125<br>2 Exp. Share SVI 174<br>2 Level Ball BST 129<br>1 Energy Search SVI 172<br>1 Hisuian Heavy Ball ASR 146<br>1 Pal Pad SVI 182<br>2 Lake Acuity LOR 160</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 13<br></strong>11 Water Energy SWSHEnergy 3<br>2 Double Turbo Energy BRS 151</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">ALTERNATE LISTS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1vDHvP68ZvE">KatanaTCG’s version of this archetype</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Testing Streams</h3>
</div>

                <img data-stretch="false" data-src="/images/60a1526c7bf87614.webp" data-image="/images/60a1526c7bf87614.webp" data-image-dimensions="550x294" data-image-focal-point="0.5,0.5" alt="" elementtiming="system-image-block" data-sqsp-image-classic-block-image="" src="/images/60a1526c7bf87614.webp" width="550" height="294" sizes="(max-width: 640px) 100vw, (max-width: 767px) 50vw, 50vw" style="display:block;object-fit: cover; width: 100%; height: 100%; object-position: 50% 50%" srcset="/images/60a1526c7bf87614.webp 100w, /images/60a1526c7bf87614.webp 300w, /images/60a1526c7bf87614.webp 500w, /images/60a1526c7bf87614.webp 750w, /images/60a1526c7bf87614.webp 1000w, /images/60a1526c7bf87614.webp 1500w, /images/60a1526c7bf87614.webp 2500w" loading="lazy" decoding="async" data-loader="sqs">

</div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-fdaef49f5f5382583408"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Using the deck’s various search cards and draw options, seek out and bench as many Pokémon as possible to boost the damage of Origin Forme Palkia VSTAR’s Subspace Swell attack which does 60 damage plus 20 more damage for each Benched Pokémon (both yours and your opponent’s). Throughout the game, discard Water Energy via Ultra Ball, Kirlia’s Refinement Ability, and Radiant Greninja’s Concealed Cards ability. As needed, use Origin Forme Palkia VSTAR’s Star Portal ability to power up either another Palkia VSTAR, Lumineon V, or Radiant Greninja. </p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ASR/40">Origin Forme Palkia VSTAR ASR 40</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ASR/46">Radiant Greninja ASR 46</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BST/40">Empoleon V BST 40</a> to slow down your opponent’s setup.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Building the deck with either the <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BRS/121">Bibarel</a> or <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SV1S/61?translate=en">Revaroom</a> engines in place of the Kirlia engine.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Pairing Palkia with <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/46">Ice Rider Calyrex VMAX CRE 46</a>.</p></li></ul>
</div>




















  
  



</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-ed81390860f6ab726bfa"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">EXAMPLE DECK LIST</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 17<br></strong>4 Kirlia SIT 68<br>4 Ralts ASR 60<br>3 Origin Forme Palkia V ASR 39<br>3 Origin Forme Palkia VSTAR ASR 40<br>1 Gallade ASR 62<br>1 Lumineon V BRS 40<br>1 Radiant Greninja ASR 46</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 35<br></strong>3 Melony CRE 146<br>2 Boss's Orders BRS 132<br>2 Irida ASR 147<br>2 Judge SVI 176<br>2 Professor's Research SVI 190<br>2 Serena SIT 164<br>4 Battle VIP Pass FST 225<br>4 Energy Search SVI 172<br>4 Ultra Ball SVI 196<br>2 Level Ball BST 129<br>2 Nest Ball SVI 181<br>2 Switch SVI 194<br>1 Energy Retrieval SVI 171<br>1 Choice Belt BRS 135<br>1 Path to the Peak CRE 148<br>1 Temple of Sinnoh ASR 155</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 8<br></strong>8 Water Energy SWSHEnergy 3</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Testing Streams</h3>
</div>

</div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1671501147277_284673"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Bench and evolve Kyurem V and Origin Forme Palkia V into Kyurem VMAX and Origin Forme Palkia STAR, respectively. After using Switching Cups to ensure that the top card of your deck is a Water Energy, attach Water Energy to Kyurem VMAX via its Glaciated World ability, which lets you discard the top card of your deck and attach it to 1 of your Pokémon if it is a Water Energy. Attach additional energy cards via manual attachments, Melony, and with Palkia VSTAR’s Star Portal VSTAR Power. Use Kyurem VMAX’s Max Frost attack, which does 120 damage plus 50 damage more for each Water Energy you discard from Kyurem VMAX. Alternatively, attack using Origin Forme Palkia VSTAR’s Subspace Swell attack, which does 60 damage plus 20 more damage for each Benched Pokémon, or Radiant Greninja’s Moonlight Shuriken attack, which does 90 damage to two of your opponent’s Pokémon.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ASR/40">Origin Forme Palkia VSTAR ASR 40</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S11/30?translate=en">Kyurem VMAX LOR 49</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/EVS/162">Switching Cups</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ASR/147">Irida</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/146">Melony</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ASR/46">Radiant Greninja ASR 46</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/137">Expedition Uniform</a> as an alternative means of manipulating the top card of your deck.</p></li></ul>
</div>




















  
  



</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1671501147277_442433"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">EXAMPLE DECK LIST</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 13<br></strong>3 Kyurem V LOR 48<br>3 Kyurem VMAX LOR 49<br>2 Origin Forme Palkia V ASR 39<br>2 Origin Forme Palkia VSTAR ASR 40<br>1 Drapion V LOR 118<br>1 Lumineon V BRS 40<br>1 Radiant Greninja ASR 46</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 36<br></strong>3 Irida ASR 147<br>3 Melony CRE 146<br>2 Boss's Orders BRS 132<br>2 Professor's Research SVI 190<br>4 Nest Ball SVI 181<br>4 Switching Cups EVS 162<br>4 Ultra Ball SVI 196<br>3 Energy Search SVI 172<br>3 Trekking Shoes CRZ 145<br>2 Escape Rope BST 125<br>1 Energy Retrieval SVI 171<br>1 Hisuian Heavy Ball ASR 146<br>1 Lost Vacuum CRZ 135<br>1 Choice Belt BRS 135<br>2 Primordial Altar SIT 161</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 11<br></strong>11 Water Energy SWSHEnergy 3</p>
</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Using the components of the Comfey engine, get first to four, then seven, and finally ten cards in the Lost Zone—use Colress’s Experiment and Comfey’s Flower Selecting Ability to do so. Attack using Cramorant and its Spit Innocently attack, which does 110 damage (without the benefit of Weakness) for 0 Energy if you have four cards in the Lost Zone, and attack using Sableye and its Lost Mine attack, which allows you to put 12 damage counters on your opponent’s Pokémon in any way you like, provided you have 10 cards in the Lost Zone.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Comfey Engine (<a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S11/49?translate=en">Comfey LOR 79</a>, <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S11/95?translate=en">Colress’s Experiment</a>, <a href="/highlights/sv1#block-yui_3_17_2_1_1670429570735_273847">Beach Court</a>)</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/LOR/50">Cramorant LOR 50</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/LOR/70">Sableye LOR 70</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SV1S/45?translate=en">Hawlucha SVI 119</a> as math fixer against certain opposing archetypes, including other Lost Box variants.</p></li></ul>
</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Bench and evolve Ice Rider Calyrex V into Ice Rider Calyrex VMAX, and bench Bidoof to prepare it to evolve on subsequent turns to take advantage of Bibarel’s Industrious Incisors ability. Use a combination of manually attaching energy and Melony to power up Ice Rider Calyrex VMAX’s two attacks, Ride of the High King and Max Lance, and use those attacks to take knockouts against your opponent’s Pokémon.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/cre/46/">Ice Rider Calyrex VMAX CRE 46</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/146/">Melony</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Building the deck with either the <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SIT/68">Kirlia</a> or <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SV1S/61?translate=en">Revaroom</a> engines in place of the Bibarel engine.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/FST/230">Cross Switcher</a> as an alternate switching and gusting effect.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/LOR/118">Drapion V LOR 118</a> as a counter to Mew VMAX.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BST/40">Empoleon V BST 40</a> to take advantage of its Emperor’s Eyes Ability to slow down your opponent’s setup with the Abilities of Pokémon like Comfey and its Flower Selecting Ability.</p></li></ul>
</div>




















  
  



</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1671501147277_407911"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">EXAMPLE DECK LIST</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 15<br></strong>4 Ice Rider Calyrex V CRE 45<br>3 Ice Rider Calyrex VMAX CRE 46<br>2 Bidoof BRS 120<br>2 Bibarel BRS 121<br>1 Lumineon V BRS 40<br>1 Origin Forme Palkia V ASR 39<br>1 Origin Forme Palkia VSTAR ASR 40<br>1 Radiant Greninja ASR 46</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 36<br></strong>4 Irida ASR 147<br>3 Melony CRE 146<br>2 Boss's Orders BRS 132<br>2 Judge SVI 176<br>1 Serena SIT 164<br>4 Energy Search SVI 172<br>4 Nest Ball SVI 181<br>4 Trekking Shoes CRZ 145<br>4 Ultra Ball SVI 196<br>1 Battle VIP Pass FST 225<br>1 Escape Rope BST 125<br>1 Hisuian Heavy Ball ASR 146<br>1 Lost Vacuum CRZ 135<br>1 Pal Pad SVI 182<br>3 Path to the Peak CRE 148</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 9<br></strong>9 Water Energy SWSHEnergy 3</p>
</div>




















  
  



</div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1674655273413_461926" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1674655273413_461926"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts. <div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Evolve Wiglett into Wugtrio and Cherubi into Cherrim. Power up Wugtrio with Cherrim’s Spring Bloom Ability, which states, “As often as you like during your turn, you may attach a [G] Energy card from your hand to 1 of your Pokémon that doesn't have a Rule Box <em>(Pokémon V, Pokémon-GX, etc. have Rule Boxes)</em>.” Attack with Wugtrio’s Sea Tunneling attack, which has you flip three coins and discard the top 3 cards of your opponent’s deck for each heads.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SV1S/20?translate=en">Wugtrio SVI 153</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BST/8">Cherrim BST 8</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRZ/142">Rescue Carrier</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/EVS/103">Zoroark EVS 103</a> as an alternate way of streaming Wugtrio to ensure you’re attacking with it each turn.</p></li></ul>
</div>




















  
  



</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1674655273413_481844"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">EXAMPLE DECK LIST</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 15<br></strong>4 Wiglett SVI 55<br>4 Wugtrio SVI 57<br>3 Cherrim BST 8<br>3 Cherubi BRS 12<br>1 Manaphy BRS 41</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 34<br></strong>4 Bruno BST 121<br>3 Professor's Research SVI 190<br>4 Energy Retrieval SVI 171<br>4 Level Ball BST 129<br>4 Nest Ball SVI 181<br>4 Rescue Carrier CRZ 142<br>3 Welcoming Lantern CRE 156<br>2 Energy Loto ASR 140<br>2 Energy Recycler BST 124<br>1 Hisuian Heavy Ball ASR 146<br>3 PokéStop PGO 68</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 11<br></strong>7 Grass Energy SWSHEnergy 1<br>4 Double Turbo Energy BRS 151</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Testing Streams</h3>
</div>




















  
  



</div></div><div class="row sqs-row"><div class="col sqs-col-3 span-3"><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-video video-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.video/a105bd59-e005-4ba7-a62b-8027aa2c6392_362/website.components.video.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.video/a105bd59-e005-4ba7-a62b-8027aa2c6392_362/4032.js&quot;,&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.video/a105bd59-e005-4ba7-a62b-8027aa2c6392_362/website.components.video.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.video" data-sqsp-block="video" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1675951991778_245615" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1675951991778_245615"><div class="sqs-block-content">

</div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1673643764670_350196"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Using Level Ball, fish each of your Tatsugiri out of the deck. Discard the Tatsugiri with Ultra Ball or with Kirlia’s Refinement Ability. Use Double Turbo Energy, Raihan or, if forced to start a Tatsugiri, Tatsugiri’s Preparations attack, to power up Dondozo’s Vent Wrath attack, which does 50 damage for each Tatsugiri in your discard pile.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SV1S/21?translate=en">Dondozo SVI 61</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SV1S/22?translate=en">Tatsugiri SVI 62</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRZ/107">Ditto CRZ 107</a> to use its Sudden Transformation Ability to copy a discarded Dondozo’s Vent Wrath attack.</p></li></ul>
</div>




















  
  



</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1673643764670_323043"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">EXAMPLE DECK LIST</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 18<br></strong>4 Dondozo SVI 61<br>4 Kirlia SIT 68<br>4 Ralts ASR 60<br>4 Tatsugiri SVI 62<br>1 Gallade ASR 62<br>1 Radiant Greninja ASR 46</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 32<br></strong>4 Professor's Research SVI 190<br>2 Boss's Orders BRS 132<br>2 Judge SVI 176<br>2 Klara CRE 145<br>2 Raihan CRZ 140<br>1 Serena SIT 164<br>4 Level Ball BST 129<br>4 Ultra Ball SVI 196<br>3 Nest Ball SVI 181<br>2 Hisuian Heavy Ball ASR 146<br>2 Switch SVI 194<br>1 Pal Pad SVI 182<br>2 Choice Belt BRS 135<br>1 Mesagoza SVI 178</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 10<br></strong>6 Water Energy SWSHEnergy 3<br>4 Double Turbo Energy BRS 151</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Testing Streams</h3>
</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Use Miraidon ex to seek Magnemite in the early game so that they can be evolved up quickly into Magnezone ex using Rare Candy. Attack with Magnezone ex’s Energy Crush attack, which does 50 damage for each Energy attached to all of your opponent's Pokémon, or with its Pulse Launcher attack which, for an additional Energy attachment, does 220 damage at the expense of putting 30 damage on the attacking Magnezone ex. Use Cheryl to heal away damage on Magnezone ex, making it more difficult for your opponent to take Prize cards.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SV1V/28?translate=en">Magnezone ex SVI 65</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SV1V/37?translate=en">Miraidon ex SVI 81</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/en/EVS/152">Raihan</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SV1V/69?translate=en">Electric Generator</a> to make it easier to power up the attacks of Miraidon ex, and to a lesser extent, Magnezone ex.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">A copy of <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BRS/40">Lumineon V BRS 40</a> to make it easier to find Arven, which can help you find Rare Candy.</p></li></ul>
</div>




















  
  



</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1668362452397_37335"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">EXAMPLE DECK LIST</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 12<br></strong>4 Magnemite SVI 63<br>4 Magnezone ex SVI 65<br>2 Miraidon ex SVI 81<br>1 Drapion V LOR 118<br>1 Radiant Greninja ASR 46</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 37<br></strong>4 Professor's Research SVI 190<br>2 Arven SVI 166<br>2 Boss's Orders BRS 132<br>2 Cheryl BST 123<br>2 Raihan CRZ 140<br>1 Serena SIT 164<br>4 Nest Ball SVI 181<br>4 Rare Candy SVI 191<br>4 Trekking Shoes CRZ 145<br>4 Ultra Ball SVI 196<br>2 Switch SVI 194<br>1 Hisuian Heavy Ball ASR 146<br>2 Choice Belt BRS 135<br>2 Beach Court SVI 167<br>1 Stormy Mountains EVS 161</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 11<br></strong>11 Lightning Energy SWSHEnergy 4</p>
</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Power up Magnezone VSTAR’s Magnet Grip attack, which does 180 damage and lets you search your deck for and 2 Item cards. Use its Electro Star VSTAR attack, which does 90 damage to 2 of your opponent’s Benched Pokémon, as either a finishing move against damaged Pokémon or as a means of picking off your opponent’s support Pokémon, like Kirlia. Use Electric Generator to help power up Magnezone VSTAR’s Magnetic Grip attack or Miraidon ex’s Photon Blaster attack.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/en/LOR/57">Magnezone VSTAR LOR 57</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SV1V/37?translate=en">Miraidon ex SVI 81</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SV1V/69?translate=en">Electric Generator</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SIT/156">Forest Seal Stone</a> as an alternative VSTAR Power option, using Star Alchemy to search out any 1 card that you may need.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SV1V/45?translate=en">Klefki SVI 96</a> to slow down your opponent’s setup with its Mischievous Lock Ability.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BRS/48">Raikou V BRS 48</a> as an alternate attacking option that’s fast to setup that Magnezone VSTAR.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BST/126">Exp. Share</a> as a means of preserving some accelerated energy.</p></li></ul>
</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Get Mareep into play as early as possible with the assistance of Miraidon ex’s Tandem Unit Ability. Evolve Mareep into Flaaffy and use Flaaffy’s Dynamotor ability or Raihan, as necessary, to attach additional energy to Miraidon ex or to Ampharos ex, as needed. Attack using Ampharos ex’s Lightning Tail attack, which does 140 damage, plus 100 more damage if you discard 2 Energy from Ampharos ex, or, attack with Miraidon ex’s Photon Blaster attack, which does 220 damage and prevents Miraidon ex from attacking next turn.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SV1V/37?translate=en">Miraidon ex SVI 81</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/EVS/55">Flaaffy EVS 55</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SVAL/7?translate=en">Ampharos ex SVP 16</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SV1V/69?translate=en">Electric Generator</a> as an additional means of energy acceleration.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SV1S/45?translate=en">Hawlucha SVI 119</a> as math fixer against certain opposing archetypes.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SV1V/77?translate=en">Miriam</a> as an alternate Pokémon recovery option.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/LOR/118">Drapion V LOR 118</a> as an additional means of combating opposing Mew VMAX and Gardevoir ex decks.</p></li></ul>
</div>

</div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1670747444166_43569"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">[Flaaffy Build] Get Mareep into play as early as possible with the assistance of Miraidon ex’s Tandem Unit Ability. Evolve Mareep into Flaaffy and use Flaaffy’s Dynamotor ability or Raihan, as necessary, to attach additional energy to Miraidon ex. Attack using Miraidon ex’s Photon Blaster attack, which does 220 damage and prevents Miraidon ex from attacking next turn. As needed, evolve a Flaaffy into Ampharos ex to boost its HP and to use its Lightning Tail attack, which does 140 damage, plus 100 more damage if you discard 2 Energy from Ampharos ex. For larger knock outs, power up Raichu V and with additional Lightning Energy in play, use its Dynamic Spark attack, which does 60 damage for each Lightning Energy you discard from any of your Pokémon.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">[Regieleki VMAX Build] With the help of Miraidon ex’s Tandem Unit Ability, get Regieleki V into play. Evolve Regieleki V into Regieleki VMAX so that Miraidon ex’s damage is boosted by Regieleki VMAX’s Transistor Ability, which adds 30 damage to the attacks of your Basic Lightning Pokémon. On early turns after using Tandem Unit, use Klefki’s Mischievous Lock to slow down your opponent’s setup.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SV1V/37?translate=en">Miraidon ex SVI 81</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SV1V/69?translate=en">Electric Generator</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">[Flaaffy Build] <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/EVS/55">Flaaffy EVS 55</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">[Flaaffy Build] <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SVAL/7?translate=en">Ampharos ex SVP 16</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">[Flaaffy Build] <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BRS/45">Raichu V BRS 45</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">[Regieleki VMAX Build] <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SIT/58">Regieleki VMAX SIT 58</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">A thin line of <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CEL/7">Flying Pikachu VMAX CEL 7</a> as a counter to single prize-heavy Lost Box decks.</p></li></ul>
</div>




















  
  



</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1676814664363_367793" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1676814664363_367793"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts. <!-- Tab content -->
<div id="Flaaffy_Miraidon" class="miraidonex_content">
<br>  
<div class="note_box">
  <h3>Card Availability Note</h3>
  <p>Ampharos ex SVP 16 was cut from Scarlet &amp; Violet base set and won't be available until the Ampharos ex Battle Deck releases on 5 May 2023. As a result, Ampharos ex won't be tournament legal until 19 May 2023.</p>
  <p>Suggested temporary stand-in: Raichu V BRS 45 or Regieleki EVS 60</p>
</div>

<h3>Example Deck List</h3>

<p><strong>Pokémon - 15</strong><br>
4 Mareep EVS 54<br>
3 Flaaffy EVS 55<br>
3 Miraidon ex SVI 81<br>
1 Ampharos ex SVP 16<br>
1 Drapion V LOR 118<br>

</div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-07d47a20131b5627fa70"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Bench Meloetta, Mew V, and Genesect V, filling your bench with Genesect V and a spare Mew V as quickly as possible. Use Genesect V’s Fusion System ability to draw cards, especially after thinning your hand with Ultra Ball to find your Pokémon or using Cram-o-matic to find specific cards in your deck. Evolve Mew V into Mew VMAX and use its Cross Fusion Strike to copy Genesect V’s Techno Blast attack. Boost Mew VMAX’s attack damage with Choice Band and Power Tablet.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S8/40/">Mew VMAX FST 114</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S8/69/">Genesect V FST 185</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S8/92/">Power Tablet</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">(Fusion Strike Energy Build: <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/FST/124">Meloetta FST 124</a>, <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S8/96/">Elesa’s Sparkle</a>, <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S8/100/">Fusion Strike Energy</a>)</p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">[Fusion Strike Build] <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/FST/42">Oricorio FST 42</a> to make it more difficult for your opponent to take Knock Outs against your Fusion Strike Pokémon, like Mew VMAX.</p></li></ul>
</div>




















  
  



</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1676818231509_87000" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1676818231509_87000"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts. </div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1671806157369_108198"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">While using Klefki’s Mischievous Lock Ability to slow down your opponent’s setup, play down and evolve Ralts into Kirlia, or with Rare Candy, into Gardevoir ex. Use Gardevoir ex Psychic Embrace Ability to attach Psychic Energy from your discard pile to your Pokémon to prepare them to attack. Use Zacian V, Wyrdeer V, Gallade, Gardevoir, and even Gardevoir ex itself to take knockouts as needed.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Kirlia Engine (<a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SIT/68">Kirlia SIT 68</a>, <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/61">Gardevoir CRE 61</a>, <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ASR/62">Gallade ASR 62</a>, <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SV1S/28?translate=en">Gardevoir ex SVI 86</a>)</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SV1V/45?translate=en">Klefki SVI 96</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CEL/16">Zacian V CEL 16</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/LOR/74">Cresselia LOR 74</a> to use its Moonglow Reverse attack to spread damage counters from your Pokémon onto your opponent’s.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SV1V/71?translate=en">Picnic Basket</a> and/or <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SIT/16">Radiant Tsareena SIT 16</a> as a means of healing off a bit of the damage caused by Psychic Embrace, to push the damage Zacian V can do a bit higher.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/EVS/63">Galarian Articuno EVS 63</a> as a snipe option with its Psylaser attack.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/81">Gallade CRE 81</a> as an alternate attacker, making use of its Dynablade attack which does 60 damage for each of your opponent’s Pokémon V in play.</p></li></ul>
</div>




















  
  



</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1671806157369_83453"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">EXAMPLE DECK LIST</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 17<br></strong>4 Kirlia SIT 68<br>3 Ralts ASR 60<br>2 Gardevoir ex SVI 86<br>2 Klefki SVI 96<br>1 Gallade ASR 62<br>1 Gardevoir CRE 61<br>1 Radiant Greninja ASR 46<br>1 Ralts SIT 67<br>1 Wyrdeer V ASR 134<br>1 Zacian V CEL 16</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 32<br></strong>4 Professor's Research SVI 190<br>2 Boss's Orders BRS 132<br>2 Judge SVI 176<br>1 Arven SVI 166<br>1 Cheryl BST 123<br>1 Miriam SVI 179<br>4 Fog Crystal CRE 140<br>4 Level Ball BST 129<br>3 Nest Ball SVI 181<br>3 Ultra Ball SVI 196<br>2 Rare Candy SVI 191<br>2 Switch SVI 194<br>1 Sky Seal Stone CRZ 143<br>2 Primordial Altar SIT 161</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 11<br></strong>11 Psychic Energy SWSHEnergy 5</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">ALTERNATE LISTS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=TobbXAY9lY8">Rare Candy’s version of this archetype</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=qa7Mc5nAcLk">NinecardTCG’s version of this archetype</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Testing Streams</h3>
</div>

</div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1673643764670_251407"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">While using Klefki’s Mischievous Lock Ability and Banette ex’s Eternal Darkness attack to slow down your opponent’s setup, play down and evolve Ralts into Kirlia, or with Rare Candy, into Gardevoir ex. Use Gardevoir ex Psychic Embrace Ability to attach Psychic Energy from your discard pile to your Pokémon to prepare them to attack. Use Gallade, Gardevoir, and even Gardevoir ex itself to take knockouts as needed while preparing Mewtwo V-UNION to come out and terrorize your opponent with its wide variety of attacks.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Kirlia Engine (<a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SIT/68">Kirlia SIT 68</a>, <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/61">Gardevoir CRE 61</a>, <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ASR/62">Gallade ASR 62</a>, <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SV1S/28?translate=en">Gardevoir ex SVI 86</a>)</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SV1V/45?translate=en">Klefki SVI 96</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp?q=Mewtwo%20V%20UNION&amp;translate=en">Mewtwo V-UNION PR-SW 159-162</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRZ/141">Rare Candy</a> and <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SV1V/76?translate=en">Arven</a> to make it quicker to evolve into Gardevoir ex.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/EVS/63">Galarian Articuno EVS 63</a> as a snipe option with its Psylaser attack.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CEL/16">Zacian V CEL 16</a> to use as an alternate attacker that’s a hard-hitting Basic Pokémon.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/81">Gallade CRE 81</a> as an alternate attacker, making use of its Dynablade attack which does 60 damage for each of your opponent’s Pokémon V in play.</p></li></ul>
</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Bench and evolve Shadow Rider Calyrex V into Shadow Rider Calyrex VMAX to make full use of its Underworld Door ability, which not only attaches a Psychic Energy from your hand to one of your benched Psychic Pokémon, it draws two cards when you do. Use either Shadow Rider Calyrex VMAX’s Max Geist attack or Zacian V’s Storm Slash attack to take knockouts.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/75/">Shadow Rider Calyrex VMAX CRE 75</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SV1V/45?translate=en">Klefki SVI 96</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CEL/16">Zacian V CEL 16</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Building Shadow Rider Calyrex VMAX as part of a Gardevoir ex archetype, using the Kirlia Engine (<a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SIT/68">Kirlia SIT 68</a>, <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/61">Gardevoir CRE 61</a>, <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ASR/62">Gallade ASR 62</a>, <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SV1S/28?translate=en">Gardevoir ex SVI 86</a>) to assist with consistency and energy acceleration.</p></li></ul>
</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY </h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Get out Houndour and Single Strike Urshifu V on early turns so that they can evolve as soon as possible. Using Houndoom's Single Strike Roar ability, search your deck for Single Strike Energy and attach them to Single Strike Urshifu VMAX’s to use its Beatdown and G-Max One Blow attacks. As necessary, take knockouts using Stonjourner’s Giga Hammer or Single Strike Urshifu V’s Impact Blow. Recycle your Single Strike Energy with Urn of Vitality.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://serebii.net/card/battlestyles/086.shtml">Single Strike Urshifu VMAX BST 86</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://serebii.net/card/battlestyles/096.shtml">Houndoom BST 96</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://serebii.net/card/singlestrikemaster/070.shtml">Single Strike Energy BST 141</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ASR/150">Roxanne</a> as a means of disrupting your opponent in the mid to late game.</p></li></ul>
</div>




















  
  



</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1671501147277_550804"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Evolve Rapid Strike Urshifu V into Rapid Strike Urshifu VMAX and use the deck’s various switching cards to hit your opponent’s Pokémon with Gale Thrust, taking selective knockouts against supporting Pokémon like Lumineon V and Miraidon ex where possible to take advantage of weakness. Boost the damage of GMAX Rapid Flow against your opponent’s benched Pokémon V with Passimian’s Throwing Coach Ability.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://serebii.net/card/battlestyles/088.shtml">Rapid Strike Urshifu VMAX BST 88</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/88/" target="">Passimian CRE 88</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Building the deck as a part of a <a href="#lugiavstar">Rapid Strike Lugia VSTAR deck</a>.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Leaning heavier into the Rapid Strike toolbox with options like <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/EVS/83">Medicham V EVS 83</a>, <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/FST/79">Inteleon VMAX FST 79</a>, <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/53">Zeraora V CRE 53</a>, <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/EVS/75">Sylveon VMAX EVS 75</a>, and <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/21">Blaziken VMAX CRE 21</a>.</p></li></ul>
</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Evolve Darkrai V into Darkrai VSTAR and use its Dark Pulse attack, which does 30 damage plus 30 more damage for each Darkness Energy attached to all of your Pokémon. To ramp up Darkrai VSTAR’s attack damage, use Galarian Moltres V’s Direflame Wings ability, which attaches a Darkness Energy from your discard pile to itself, and use Dark Patch to rapidly attach Energy to your benched Darkness Pokémon.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="/highlights/ss10#block-6e5d44ded95dfb5bdd41">Darkrai VSTAR ASR 99</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="/highlights/ss10#block-7f3375e2383c96f73ef9">Dark Patch ASR 139</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/cre/97">Galarian Moltres V CRE 97</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/EVS/93">Galarian Moltres EVS 93</a> and an additional copy of Klara to take full advantage of its Fiery Wrath attack which does 20 damage plus 50 more damage for each Prize card your opponent has taken.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">The <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BRS/121">Bibarel engine</a> in place of the Revavroom engine.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/FST/230">Cross Switcher</a> as an alternate switching and gusting effect.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/FST/235">Judge</a> or <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ASR/150">Roxanne</a> as a means of disrupting your opponent’s hand, especially in the mid to late game.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ASR/146">Hisuian Heavy Ball</a> to fish Prized copies of Darkrai V or Radiant Greninja out of your Prize cards.</p></li></ul>
</div>




















  
  



</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1671501147277_627262"><div class="sqs-block-content">

</div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1671501147277_328823"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Using Magnezone’s Giga Magnet Ability and manual attachments, prepare to take knockouts with Origin Forme Dialga VSTAR’s Metal Blast, which does 40 damage plus 40 more damage for each Metal Energy attached to Origin Forme Dialga VSTAR or its VSTAR Power, Chronos Star, which does 220 damage and allows you to immediately take a second turn after attacking.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="/highlights/ss10#block-yui_3_17_2_1_1646379463170_1683133">Origin Forme Dialga VSTAR ASR 114</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ASR/107">Magnezone ASR 107</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ASR/107">Miraidon ex SVI 81</a> to help search out your Magnemite so that they can more quickly evolve into Magnezone.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Building the deck around the Lost Engine instead of Magnezone.</p></li></ul>
</div>




















  
  



</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1671501147277_666673"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">EXAMPLE DECK LIST</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 13<br></strong>3 Magnemite SVI 63<br>3 Magnezone ASR 107<br>3 Origin Forme Dialga V ASR 113<br>3 Origin Forme Dialga VSTAR ASR 114<br>1 Radiant Greninja ASR 46</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 29<br></strong>4 Adaman ASR 135<br>2 Boss's Orders BRS 132<br>1 Avery CRE 130<br>1 Serena SIT 164<br>4 Nest Ball SVI 181<br>4 Pokégear 3.0 SVI 186<br>3 Rare Candy SVI 191<br>3 Ultra Ball SVI 196<br>2 Battle VIP Pass FST 225<br>2 Energy Recycler BST 124<br>1 Hisuian Heavy Ball ASR 146<br>2 Mesagoza SVI 178</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 18<br></strong>18 Metal Energy SWSHEnergy 8</p>
</div>

</div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1674655273413_733517"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Get Rayquaza V and Mareep into play as early as possible, evolving into Rayquaza VMAX and Flaaffy as the opportunity presents. Manually attach Fire energy to Rayquaza VMAX and use Flaaffy’s Dynamotor ability or Raihan, as necessary, to attach additional energy to Rayquaza VMAX. Attack using Rayquaza VMAX’s Max Burst attack, discarding Lightning energy attached to the attacking Rayquaza VMAX to do 80 damage for each Lightning Energy discarded.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7R/46/">Rayquaza V EVS 110</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7R/47/">Rayquaza VMAX 111</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7R/27/">Flaaffy EVS 55</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BRS/45">Raichu V BRS 45</a> as an additional attacker that can take big knock outs against your opponent’s Pokémon.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/145">Klara</a> and/or <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BST/124">Energy Recycler</a>, to make it possible to reuse Fire Energy as possible.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/PGO/11">Radiant Charizard PGO 11</a> as a mid to late game single prize attacker.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">A Fire-heavy build instead of a Lightning-heavy build, using <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BRS/144">Magma Basin</a> and <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SV1S/12?translate=en">Armarouge</a> to get Rayquaza VMAX powered up.</p></li></ul>
</div>




















  
  



</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1676814664363_392370" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1676814664363_392370"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts.     <div class="note_box">
  <h3>Card Availability Note</h3>
  <p>Ampharos ex SVP 16 was cut from Scarlet &amp; Violet base set and won't be available until the Ampharos ex Battle Deck releases on 5 May 2023. As a result, Ampharos ex won't be tournament legal until 19 May 2023.</p>
  <p>Suggested temporary stand-in: Raichu V BRS 45</p>
</div>

  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">EXAMPLE DECK LIST</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 18<br></strong>4 Flaaffy EVS 55<br>4 Mareep CRE 47<br>3 Rayquaza V EVS 110<br>3 Rayquaza VMAX EVS 111<br>2 Miraidon ex SVI 81<br>1 Ampharos ex SVP 16<br>1 Radiant Greninja ASR 46</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 30<br></strong>4 Professor's Research SVI 190<br>2 Boss's Orders BRS 132<br>2 Serena SIT 164<br>1 Raihan CRZ 140<br>4 Level Ball BST 129<br>4 Ultra Ball SVI 196<br>3 Nest Ball SVI 181<br>2 Escape Rope BST 125<br>2 Switch SVI 194<br>2 Trekking Shoes CRZ 145<br>2 Forest Seal Stone SIT 156<br>1 Beach Court SVI 167<br>1 Stormy Mountains EVS 161</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 12<br></strong>8 Lightning Energy SWSHEnergy 4<br>4 Fire Energy SWSHEnergy 2</p>
</div>

                <img data-stretch="false" data-src="/images/462cce726aa51c71.webp" data-image="/images/462cce726aa51c71.webp" data-image-dimensions="550x294" data-image-focal-point="0.5,0.5" alt="" elementtiming="system-image-block" data-sqsp-image-classic-block-image="" src="/images/462cce726aa51c71.webp" width="550" height="294" sizes="(max-width: 640px) 100vw, (max-width: 767px) 50vw, 50vw" style="display:block;object-fit: cover; width: 100%; height: 100%; object-position: 50% 50%" srcset="/images/462cce726aa51c71.webp 100w, /images/462cce726aa51c71.webp 300w, /images/462cce726aa51c71.webp 500w, /images/462cce726aa51c71.webp 750w, /images/462cce726aa51c71.webp 1000w, /images/462cce726aa51c71.webp 1500w, /images/462cce726aa51c71.webp 2500w" loading="lazy" decoding="async" data-loader="sqs">
