</div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-39519a44c578e32e5f98"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Using the components of the Comfey engine, get first to four, then seven, and finally ten cards in the Lost Zone—use Colress’s Experiment and Comfey’s Flower Selecting Ability to do so. Attack using Cramorant and its Spit Innocently attack, which does 110 damage (without the benefit of Weakness) for 0 Energy if you have four cards in the Lost Zone, and attack using Sableye and its Lost Mine attack, which allows you to put 12 damage counters on your opponent’s Pokémon in any way you like, provided you have 10 cards in the Lost Zone. Use Mirage Gate to power up Giratina VSTAR’s attacks and take knockouts with its Lost Impact and Star Requiem attacks.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Comfey Engine (<a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S11/49?translate=en">Comfey LOR 79</a>, <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S11/95?translate=en">Colress’s Experiment</a>, <a href="/highlights/sv1#block-yui_3_17_2_1_1670429570735_273847">Beach Court</a>)</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/LOR/50">Cramorant LOR 50</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/LOR/70">Sableye LOR 70</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/148">Path to the Peak</a> or <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ASR/155">Temple of Sinnoh</a> as Stadium-based disruption options.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ASR/146">Hisuian Heavy Ball</a> to fish out Prized copies of Giratina V, Radiant Greninja, or other 1-of basic Pokémon inclusions.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/EVS/152">Raihan</a> as an additional means of recovering and accelerating basic Energy, with the benefit of being able to search your deck for any card.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ASR/150">Roxanne</a> as an additional means of disrupting your opponent in the mid to late game.</p></li></ul>
</div>




















  
  



</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-1d824a14543c5938b51a"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">[Arceus VSTAR Version] Play down Arceus V and Hisuian Goodra V, using Arceus V’s Trinity Charge attack to power up a benched Hisuian Goodra V, and evolve Hisuian Goodra V into Hisuian Goodra VSTAR. Attack with Hisuian Goodra VSTAR’s Iron Press attack, which does 200 damage and reduces the damage Hisuian Goodra VSTAR takes during your opponent’s next turn by 80. Heal damage taken with the effect of healings cards, like Crystal Cave.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S10a/57?translate=en">Hisuian Goodra VSTAR LOR 136</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">[Arceus VSTAR Version] <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/brs/122">Arceus V BRS 122</a>, <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/brs/123">Arceus VSTAR BRS 123</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ASR/155">Temple of Sinnoh</a> to slow down Special Energy-dependent decks, like Mew VMAX or Lugia VSTAR.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ASR/153">Sweet Honey</a> or <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/EVS/150">Lucky Ice Pop</a> as an additional healing option.</p></li></ul>
</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Play down Arceus V and Duraludon V, using Arceus V’s Trinity Charge attack to power up a benched Duraludon V, and evolve Duraludon V into Duraludon VMAX. Manually attach energy to Duraludon VMAX as necessary to prepare it to attack with its G-Max Pulverization attack, which does 220 damage and goes through any walling effects on your opponent’s Active Pokémon.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7D/49/">Duraludon VMAX EVS 123</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/brs/122">Arceus V BRS 122</a>, <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/brs/123">Arceus VSTAR BRS 123</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ASR/153">Sweet Honey</a> as an additional healing option.</p></li></ul>
</div>

  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">EXAMPLE DECK LIST</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 12<br></strong>4 Arceus V BRS 122<br>3 Duraludon V EVS 122<br>3 Duraludon VMAX EVS 123<br>2 Arceus VSTAR BRS 123</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 35<br></strong>3 Judge SVI 176<br>3 Professor's Research SVI 190<br>2 Boss's Orders BRS 132<br>2 Colress's Experiment LOR 155<br>2 Serena SIT 164<br>1 Raihan CRZ 140<br>4 Lucky Ice Pop EVS 150<br>4 Nest Ball SVI 181<br>4 Ultra Ball SVI 196<br>3 Trekking Shoes CRZ 145<br>2 Switch SVI 194<br>1 Escape Rope BST 125<br>4 Crystal Cave EVS 144</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 13<br></strong>6 Metal Energy SWSHEnergy 8<br>4 Double Turbo Energy BRS 151<br>3 Fighting Energy SWSHEnergy 6</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Testing Streams</h3>
</div>

</div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1668362452397_136899"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Using Ultra Ball and Professor’s Research, Discard your Dragon-type attackers that aren’t Regidrago V or VSTAR. Attach Double Turbo Energy to Arceus V and use its Trinity Charge attack to search your deck for up to 3 Basic Energy cards and attach them to your Pokémon V in any way you like—most likely to your Regidrago V or VSTAR so that, once fully evolved in can use its Apex Dragon attack, which lets you copy the attack of any Dragon-type Pokémon in your discard pile for the cost of two Grass Energy and one Fire Energy.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BRS/122">Arceus V BRS 122</a> and <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BRS/123">Arceus VSTAR 123</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SIT/136">Regidrago VSTAR SIT 136</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Dragon-Type Attackers (<a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/EVS/123">Duraludon VMAX EVS 123</a>, <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/LOR/131">Giratina VSTAR LOR 136</a>, <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/LOR/136">Hisuian Goodra VSTAR LOR 136</a>)</p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ASR/117">Garchomp V ASR 117</a> to copy its Sonic Strike attack, which does 220 damage to 1 of your opponent’s Pokémon.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BRS/106">Flygon V BRS 106</a> to copy its Draconic Impulse attack, which does 160 damage plus 160 damage if your opponent’s Active Pokémon is a Pokémon VMAX.</p></li></ul>
</div>




















  
  



</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1668362452397_121119"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">EXAMPLE DECK LIST</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 20<br></strong>4 Arceus V BRS 122<br>2 Arceus VSTAR BRS 123<br>2 Bibarel BRS 121<br>2 Bidoof BRS 120<br>2 Regidrago V SIT 135<br>2 Regidrago VSTAR SIT 136<br>1 Drapion V LOR 118<br>1 Duraludon VMAX EVS 123<br>1 Giratina VSTAR LOR 131<br>1 Hisuian Goodra VSTAR LOR 136<br>1 Lumineon V BRS 40<br>1 Radiant Charizard CRZ 20</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 27<br></strong>3 Professor's Research SVI 190<br>2 Boss's Orders BRS 132<br>2 Judge SVI 176<br>2 Serena SIT 164<br>1 Raihan CRZ 140<br>4 Ultra Ball SVI 196<br>3 Nest Ball SVI 181<br>2 Great Ball CRZ 132<br>2 Switch SVI 194<br>2 Choice Belt BRS 135<br>2 Mesagoza SVI 178<br>2 Path to the Peak CRE 148</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 13<br></strong>6 Grass Energy SWSHEnergy 1<br>4 Double Turbo Energy BRS 151<br>3 Fire Energy SWSHEnergy 2</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">ALTERNATE LISTS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=MmevlFC4yNk">LittleDarkFury’s version of this archetype</a></p></li></ul>
</div>

</div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1671501147277_343561"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Bench Blissey V and attach an energy to it to prepare its Blissful Blast attack, using various means of discarding special energy, like Radiant Greninja’s Concealed Cards ability and Professor’s Research to dump energy into the discard pile so that it can be attached with Blissful Blast, which does 10 damage plus 30 more damage for each energy attached to Blissey and attaches up to 3 Energy cards from your discard pile to Blissey V to further boost its damage. Heal away damage on it using Lucky Ice Pop and Cheren’s Care. Slow your opponent down with disruption options like Judge. Use Miltank as necessary to go up against opposing Pokémon V as either a wall or an attacker.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.serebii.net/card/jetblackpoltergeist/052.shtml">Blissey V CRE 119</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ASR/126">Miltank ASR 126</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ASR/153">Sweet Honey</a> as an additional healing option.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Water Energy and <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/LOR/160">Lake Acuity</a>, to provide an additional bit of damage reduction for Blissey V.</p></li></ul>
</div>




















  
  



</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1671501147277_748276"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">EXAMPLE DECK LIST</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 10</strong><br>4 Blissey V CRE 119<br>3 Yveltal CEL 19<br>2 Miltank ASR 126<br>1 Radiant Greninja ASR 46</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 35</strong><br>4 Judge SVI 176<br>3 Professor's Research SVI 190<br>2 Boss's Orders BRS 132<br>2 Cheren's Care BRS 134<br>2 Serena SIT 164<br>4 Lucky Ice Pop EVS 150<br>4 Nest Ball SVI 181<br>4 Trekking Shoes CRZ 145<br>2 Echoing Horn CRE 136<br>2 Escape Rope BST 125<br>2 Choice Belt BRS 135<br>4 Path to the Peak CRE 148</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 15</strong><br>4 Double Turbo Energy BRS 151<br>4 Lucky Energy CRE 158<br>4 V Guard Energy SIT 169<br>3 Gift Energy LOR 171</p>
</div>

</div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1668362452397_157635"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Get two Archeops into the discard pile while benching and eventually evolving Lugia V into Lugia VSTAR. Prioritize getting two Archeops into play using Lugia’s Summoning Star VSTAR Power, which puts two Colorless Pokémon from your discard pile directly onto your bench. Use Archeops’ Primal Turbo Ability and manual attachments to power up Lugia VSTAR’s Tempest Dive attack or the attacks of the deck’s various attacking Pokémon.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="/highlights/ss12#block-yui_3_17_2_1_1663488163133_261232">Lugia VSTAR SIT 139</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="/highlights/ss12#block-yui_3_17_2_1_1663488163133_198080">Archeops SIT 147</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Special Energy (<a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BRS/151">Double Turbo Energy</a>, <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SIT/169">V Guard Energy</a>, Etc.)</p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Depending on the variant, <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SV1V/78?translate=en">Mesagoza</a> as an additional Pokémon search option or <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BST/137">Tower of Darkness</a> as an additional draw option.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SIT/16">Radiant Tsareena SIT 16</a> as an alternative Radiant option to use its Elegant Heal Ability, which reads, “Once during your turn, you may heal 20 damage from each of your Pokémon.”</p></li></ul>
</div>

</div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1671501147277_358684"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Play down Regigigas V and Ralts, then evolve Ralts into Kirlia to take advantage of its Refinement Ability, which lets you discard a card from your hand to draw two cards. Evolve Regigigas V into Regigigas VSTAR and use a combination of Melony and Double Turbo Energy to power up its Giga Imapact attack, which does 230 damage. Reduce the damage taken by Regigigas VSTAR with the decks various damage reduction options. Scoop Regigigas VSTAR back into your hand to avoid it giving up Prize cards with Cheren’s Care.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.pokebeach.com/2022/11/new-vstar-universe-cards-revealed-including-hatterene-vmax">Regigigas V CRZ 113</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="/highlights/ss125#block-yui_3_17_2_1_1668180913535_166843">Regigigas VSTAR CRZ 114</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/146">Melony</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Damage Reduction, Healing, and HP Boost Effects (<a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/LOR/69">Radiant Gardevoir LOR 69</a>, <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BRS/134">Cheren’s Care</a>, <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SIT/169">V Guard Energy</a>)</p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SIT/160">Leafy Camo Poncho</a> to preserve benched Regigigas VSTAR.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Building with <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BRS/123">Arceus VSTAR BRS 123</a> to take advantage of its Starbirth VSTAR Power and the energy acceleration it provides with Trinity Nova.</p></li></ul>
</div>




















  
  



</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1671501147277_831317"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">EXAMPLE DECK LIST</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 18<br></strong>4 Ralts ASR 60<br>4 Kirlia SIT 68<br>1 Gallade ASR 62<br>4 Regigigas V CRZ 113<br>3 Regigigas VSTAR CRZ 114<br>1 Lumineon V BRS 40<br>1 Radiant Gardevoir LOR 69</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 30<br></strong>4 Judge SVI 176<br>3 Melony CRE 146<br>2 Serena SIT 164<br>1 Cheren's Care BRS 134<br>4 Nest Ball SVI 181<br>3 Energy Search SVI 172<br>3 Level Ball BST 129<br>3 Ultra Ball SVI 196<br>1 Escape Rope BST 125<br>1 Pal Pad SVI 182<br>1 Switch SVI 194<br>1 Forest Seal Stone SIT 156<br>3 Path to the Peak CRE 148</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 12<br></strong>6 Water Energy SWSHEnergy 3<br>4 Double Turbo Energy BRS 151<br>2 V Guard Energy SIT 169</p>
</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Use Arceus V’s Trinity Charge attack to power up another Arceus V or VSTAR. Evolve Arceus V into Arceus VSTAR, using Trinity Nova to take knock outs and its Starbirth VSTAR Power to get any two cards from your deck when you need them. Use another Arceus VSTAR to clean up what is left of your opponent’s field after Arceus VSTAR is knocked out.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/brs/122">Arceus V BRS 122</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/brs/123">Arceus VSTAR BRS 123</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BRS/151">Double Turbo Energy</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Building around the <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BRS/121">Bibarel</a> engine instead of the Kirlia engine.</p></li></ul>
</div>




















  
  



</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1674655273413_876475"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">EXAMPLE DECK LIST</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 19<br></strong>4 Arceus V BRS 122<br>3 Arceus VSTAR BRS 123<br>3 Ralts ASR 60<br>1 Ralts SIT 67<br>4 Kirlia SIT 68<br>2 Gallade ASR 62<br>1 Gallade CRE 81<br>1 Radiant Gardevoir LOR 69</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 31<br></strong>3 Professor's Research SVI 190<br>2 Boss's Orders BRS 132<br>2 Cheren's Care BRS 134<br>2 Judge SVI 176<br>1 Avery CRE 130<br>1 Raihan CRZ 140<br>4 Nest Ball SVI 181<br>4 Ultra Ball SVI 196<br>3 Level Ball BST 129<br>3 Trekking Shoes CRZ 145<br>2 Switch SVI 194<br>1 Pal Pad SVI 182<br>2 Path to the Peak CRE 148<br>1 Collapsed Stadium BRS 137</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 10<br></strong>6 Psychic Energy SWSHEnergy 5<br>4 Double Turbo Energy BRS 151</p>
</div>




















  
  



</div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1671501147277_119805" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1671501147277_119805"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts. </div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1671501147277_366336"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Build up your bench, with Gapejaw Bog in play if possible (and/or with the help of Gengar’s Netherworld Gate Ability) and use Damage Pump to ensure that each of your Pokémon has at least a single damage counter on it. Take knock outs with Hisuian Zoroark VSTAR’s Shredding Curse attack, which does 50 damage for each of your Pokémon that has any damage counters on it.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S10a/62?translate=en">Hisuian Zoroark VSTAR LOR 147</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S10a/23?translate=en">Gengar LOR 66</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S10a/64?translate=en">Damage Pump</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ASR/142">Gapejaw Bog</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Building the deck with the <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SIT/68">Kirlia</a> and <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SV1S/28?translate=en">Gardevoir ex</a> engine, so that you can abuse Gardevoir ex’s ability to place damage counters on them or on Gengar to boost Hisuian Zoroark VSTAR’s damage.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRZ/51">Radiant Charjabug CRZ 51</a> as an alternative means of putting damage counters on Hisuian Zoroark VSTAR.</p></li></ul>
</div>




















  
  



</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1671501147277_874159"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">EXAMPLE DECK LIST</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 18</strong><br>4 Hisuian Zoroark V LOR 146<br>4 Hisuian Zoroark VSTAR LOR 147<br>2 Bidoof BRS 120<br>2 Bibarel BRS 121<br>2 Empoleon V BST 40<br>2 Gengar LOR 66<br>1 Lumineon V BRS 40<br>1 Radiant Greninja ASR 46</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 32</strong><br>3 Melony CRE 146<br>3 Professor's Research SVI 190<br>2 Boss's Orders BRS 132<br>1 Avery CRE 130<br>4 Damage Pump LOR 156<br>4 Great Ball CRZ 132<br>4 Ultra Ball SVI 196<br>2 Capturing Aroma SIT 153<br>2 Switch SVI 194<br>1 Escape Rope BST 125<br>2 Choice Belt BRS 135<br>4 Gapejaw Bog ASR 142</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 10</strong><br>6 Water Energy SWSHEnergy 3<br>4 Double Turbo Energy BRS 151</p>
</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Evolve Lechonk into Oinkologne ex and attack using its Fragrance Fury attack, which does 30 damage for each Pokémon on your opponent’s bench. Alternatively, attack with Oinkologne ex’s Heavy Stamp attack, which does 210 damage. Reduce the damage Oinkolonge ex takes using Radiant Gardevoir, Full Face Guard, and V Guard Energy, and sweep away damage on your Oinkologne ex with Cheryl or Cheren’s Care.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SV1S/68?translate=en">Oinkologne ex SVI 156</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Damage Reducers (<a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/LOR/69">Radiant Gardevoir LOR 69</a>, <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/EVS/148">Full Face Guard</a>, <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SIT/169">V Guard Energy</a>)</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Healing Options (<a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BRS/134">Cheren’s Care</a>, <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BST/123">Cheryl</a>)</p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ASR/150">Roxanne</a> as an additional means of disrupting your opponent in the mid to late game.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/LOR/118">Drapion V LOR 118</a> as an additional means of combating opposing Mew VMAX and Gardevoir ex decks.</p></li></ul>
</div>




















  
  



</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1674655273413_568811"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">EXAMPLE DECK LIST</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 15<br></strong>4 Lechonk SVI 154<br>3 Oinkologne ex SVI 156<br>2 Bibarel BRS 121<br>2 Bidoof BRS 120<br>1 Dunsparce FST 207<br>1 Manaphy BRS 41<br>1 Radiant Gardevoir LOR 69<br>1 Skwovet SVI 151</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 35<br></strong>3 Judge SVI 176<br>3 Professor's Research SVI 190<br>2 Boss's Orders BRS 132<br>2 Cheren's Care BRS 134<br>2 Cheryl BST 123<br>1 Serena SIT 164<br>4 Nest Ball SVI 181<br>4 Pokégear 3.0 SVI 186<br>4 Ultra Ball SVI 196<br>3 Switch SVI 194<br>1 Pal Pad SVI 182<br>3 Full Face Guard EVS 148<br>3 Path to the Peak CRE 148</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 10<br></strong>4 V Guard Energy SIT 169<br>3 Double Turbo Energy BRS 151<br>3 Lucky Energy CRE 158</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Alternate Lists</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ucDAcJ3wz1Y">Andrew Mahone’s version of the archetype</a></p></li></ul>
</div>

</div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1673643764670_219060"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Swarm the board with 4 Tandemaus and a Zorua or two, evolving Tandemaus up into Maushold as soon as possible. Attack with Tandemaus’ Family Attack, which does 70 damage for each Maushold you have in play. Recover Knocked Out Maushold using Zoroark’s Phantom Transformation Ability, swapping your Zoroark for a Maushold in your discard pile. Use Double Turbo Energy and Armarouge’s Fire Alarm Ability in combination with Magma Basin to power up Maushold’s Family Attack attack.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SV1V/66?translate=en">Maushold SVI 160</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/EVS/103">Zoroark EVS 103</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SV1S/12?translate=en">Armarouge SVI 41</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BRS/151">Double Turbo Energy</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BRS/132">Boss’s Orders</a> as a more universal gusting option.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/EVS/55">Flaaffy EVS 55</a> in place of Magma Basin and Armarouge, opening up options like Mesagoza to help search for Pokémon, with the tradeoff being losing the option to make use of Radiant Charizard.</p></li></ul>
</div>




















  
  



</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-border-radii="{&quot;topLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;topRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomLeft&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0},&quot;bottomRight&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;value&quot;:0.0}}" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1673643764670_193470"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">EXAMPLE DECK LIST</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 22</strong><br>4 Tandemaus SVI 160<br>4 Maushold SVI 161<br>4 Zoroark EVS 103<br>4 Zorua EVS 102<br>1 Armarouge SVI 41<br>1 Bibarel BRS 121<br>1 Manaphy BRS 41<br>1 Mightyena ASR 96<br>1 Radiant Charizard CRZ 20<br>1 Slowbro PGO 20</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 30</strong><br>4 Professor's Research SVI 190<br>3 Klara CRE 145<br>3 Serena SIT 164<br>1 Peonia CRE 149<br>4 Level Ball BST 129<br>4 Nest Ball SVI 181<br>4 Trekking Shoes CRZ 145<br>4 Ultra Ball SVI 196<br>3 Magma Basin BRS 144</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 8</strong><br>4 Double Turbo Energy BRS 151<br>4 Fire Energy SWSHEnergy 2</p>
</div>
